Our results provide a practical route to lower-overhead, context-preserving fidelity estimation for continuously parameterized quantum gates.

</details>


### [125] [Know Your Qubits, Know Your Users: Personas for Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18598)
*Lukas Schmidbauer,Joshua Ammermann,Laura Schulz,Jose Garcia-Alonso,Robert Wille,Sebastian Feld,Ina Schaefer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过专家焦点小组和实践者访谈，识别出量子软件的十一个用户角色，旨在根据利益相关者的需求设计更符合实际应用的量子软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于量子计算领域的跨学科性，需要理解不同利益相关者的观点和需求，以在抽象层次与硬件细节之间取得平衡。

Method: 在2024年Dagstuhl研讨会和2025年IEEE QCE会议上，通过专家焦点小组和定性访谈进行利益相关者分析，并提取用户 personas。

Result: 研究得出了十一个量子软件用户和利益相关者的 personas，涵盖了其用例、兴趣、限制和所需的抽象层次。

Conclusion: 基于利益相关者的 persona 分析有助于开发更贴近用户需求的、量身定制的量子软件系统。

Abstract: The advancement of quantum hardware and the intricacies of quantum computing make well-designed quantum software increasingly necessary. Due to the interdisciplinarity of the field, it is crucial to understand the perspectives and specific needs of involved stakeholders, for example, to balance the desired level of abstraction with the exposition of (hardware)-specific details. In this work, we conduct a stakeholder-based analysis to identify personas of quantum software as a means of creating meaningful, user-tailored quantum software. We conducted an expert focus group at a Dagstuhl seminar in 2024 and qualitative interviews with practitioners at conference IEEE QCE in 2025, from which we derive eleven personas of potential users and stakeholders for quantum software. We discuss these personas regarding their use cases, interests, constraints and abstraction level.

</details>


### [126] [Inverse Feshbach's problem: Solvability and solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18600)
*Miloslav Znojil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了从Feshbach有效哈密顿量的特定矩阵形式出发，通过求解一组耦合的多项式代数方程，重构全空间哈密顿量的问题，并利用计算机辅助符号操作实现了在较小维度差下的显式代数重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决如何由低维有效哈密顿量精确反推出高维全空间哈密顿量的问题，以拓展对量子系统结构的理解与建模能力。

Method: 通过建立并求解一组耦合的多项式代数方程，结合符号计算工具实现H^(N)的代数重构。

Result: 在K = N-M不大的情况下，实现了H^(N)的显式代数重构，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为从有效模型反推完整哈密顿量提供了可行的代数途径，尤其适用于低维到中等高维系统的重构问题。

Abstract: Given a certain specific, $M$ by $M$ matrix form of the Feshbach's effective (i.e., energy-dependent) Hamiltonian $H^{(M)}_{e\!f\!f}(E)$, the inverse-problem reconstruction of the full-space, $N$ by $N$ matrix Hamiltonian $H^{(N)}$ is considered and reduced to the solution of a coupled set of polynomial algebraic equations. Using computer-assisted symbolic manipulations, an explicit algebraic reconstruction of $H^{(N)}$ is found feasible at not too large $K = N-M$.

</details>


### [127] [(Almost) quadruply optimal unitary designs in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18650)
*Guoding Liu,Jonas Helsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了1维系统中的n量子比特近似酉k-设计，电路深度和魔法门数量接近已知下界，通过改进现有方法实现了多参数的近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在1维量子系统中高效构造近似酉k-设计，并尽可能接近已知的电路深度和资源下界。

Method: 结合并改进Zhang等人和Chen等人的工作：将打破Clifford对称性所需的魔法块大小从O(k log k)减少到O(log k)，并改进Chen等人的构造以实现常数谱隙的1维局部常深度电路生成酉群，再通过拼接引理组合结果。

Result: 实现了电路深度为O(log(n/ε) + k log k)，需要O(nk log k)个魔法门，接近已知下界Ω(log(n/ε) + k)和Õ(nk)，并在O(log k)因子内达到多参数近最优；同时给出了Clifford群的一个常数大小1维局部生成集。

Conclusion: 该工作在1维系统中实现了近似酉k-设计的近乎最优构造，对量子随机性和复杂性理论具有重要意义。

Abstract: We construct $n$-qubit approximate unitary $k$-designs in 1D systems, achieving circuit depth $O(\log(n/\varepsilon) + k\log k)$ with relative error $\varepsilon$ and requiring $O(nk\log k)$ magic gates. This matches existing lower bounds $Ω(\log(n/\varepsilon) + k)$ for circuit depth, and $\widetildeΩ(nk)$ for the required number of $T$ gates, up to a $\log k$ factor, achieving simultaneous near-optimality in all parameters. Our construction is based on a combination and refinement of two existing results. We reduce the required magic block size for breaking Clifford symmetries in the magic-augmented circuit construction of Zhang et al. from $O(k\log k)$ to $O(\log k)$. We also improve the breakthrough construction of Chen et al. to construct a generating set of 1D local constant-depth circuits for the unitary group with a constant spectral gap, making $O(\log k)$-local random unitaries realizable in depth $O(k\log k)$. As a by-product, we provide a constant-size 1D-local generating set for the Clifford group, which we expect to be of independent interest. Combining the two results with the gluing lemma, we prove the final result.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum-Logic Tsetlin Machines: Interpretable Quantum Machine Learning with Commuting Projector Clauses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18659)
*Krishna Bhatia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子逻辑Tsetlin机（QL-TM），将布尔文字替换为由投影算符表示的量子命题，同时保留经典的包含/排除自动机机制。在对角计算基上下文中，该模型可精确还原为普通的布尔TM子句，并与稳定子和综合征语义建立联系。实验表明，正确的非对角上下文能够恢复物理上有意义的子句，而对角或错误的上下文则丢失相位/综合征信息。研究为Tsetlin子句学习与量子逻辑之间建立了可控的桥梁，而非宣称具有量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将Tsetlin机的可解释布尔子句学习扩展到量子领域，探索其在量子逻辑环境下的适用性与表达能力，建立经典学习机制与量子逻辑之间的联系。

Method: 用投影算符表示的量子命题替代布尔文字，限制子句在可交换测量上下文中，并通过联合投影算符的Born概率激活子句；保留Tsetlin机的包含/排除状态自动机结构。

Result: 证明了在对角计算基上下文中可精确还原为传统布尔TM子句；Pauli投影子句与稳定子及综合征语义相关联；实验显示正确非对角上下文能恢复物理有意义的子句，而错误上下文则丢失关键信息；上下文预算结果符合预期的可分性阶梯规律2^(b-k)。

Conclusion: QL-TM成功构建了Tsetlin子句学习与量子逻辑之间的可控桥梁，能够在量子环境中学习可解释的逻辑结构，但重点在于机制连接而非实现量子优势。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machines (TMs) learn interpretable Boolean clauses using finite-state automata. We introduce the Quantum-Logic Tsetlin Machine (QL-TM), which replaces Boolean literals with quantum propositions represented by projectors while retaining classical include/exclude automata. Clauses are restricted to commuting measurement contexts and activate through the Born probability of their joint projector. We prove an exact reduction to ordinary Boolean TM clauses in diagonal computational-basis contexts and connect Pauli-projector clauses to stabilizer and syndrome semantics. Controlled experiments on Bell states, phase-flip syndromes, randomized 16-class stabilizer tasks, mixed literal pools, context-budget ablations, and finite-shot noise show that correct non-diagonal contexts recover physically meaningful clauses, while diagonal or wrong contexts lose the relevant phase/syndrome information. The context-budget results closely follow the predicted separability ladder 2^(b-k) as true stabilizer generators are removed. The contribution is a controlled bridge between Tsetlin clause learning and quantum logic, not a claim of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [129] [Experimental zero-added-loss multiplexing Bell-pair source for long-haul quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18666)
*Yoshiaki Tsujimoto,Daiki Ichii,Rikizo Ikuta,Mikio Fujiwara,Masahiro Takeoka,Go Kato,Kentaro Wakui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究实现了基于时间-频率复用的零附加损耗复用（ZALM）方案中的关键步骤——在16个并行频率模式间进行高保真度纠缠交换，平均保真度达93.9±1.4%，并在3.0 GHz泵浦重复频率下实现总交换速率达5.38±0.17对/秒，对应ZALM贝尔对生成速率为820对/秒，为构建高速量子中继和长距离量子网络提供了可扩展的技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高量子网络的通信速率是量子信息科学的核心挑战之一。传统纠缠分发方案受限于效率和损耗，亟需一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。ZALM方案虽被提出，但其依赖的高保真纠缠交换在密集复用的时间-频率模式上尚未实现实验验证，构成主要瓶颈。

Method: 采用时间-频率复用技术，在16个并行频率通道中实现纠缠交换；利用商用50-GHz密集波分复用（DWDM）滤波器生成光谱单模的偏振纠缠光子对，避免额外窄带滤波带来的损耗；通过3.0 GHz高重频泵浦脉冲充分利用时间自由度，提升系统速率。

Result: 实现了16个频率模式间的纠缠交换，平均保真度为93.9±1.4%；系统总交换速率达到5.38±0.17对/秒，对应ZALM贝尔对速率为820对/秒；验证了无需额外窄带滤波即可实现高质量纠缠的操作可行性。

Conclusion: 该实验成功展示了ZALM方案所需的关键技术能力，证明了结合时间与频率复用实现高保真、高速纠缠分发的可行性，为未来构建可扩展的高效率量子中继器和长距离量子网络提供了实用化路径。

Abstract: Boosting the communication rate of quantum networks is a central challenge in quantum information science. Recently, an efficient entanglement distribution scheme employing quasi-deterministic Bell-pair sources based on time-frequency multiplexing, referred to as zero-added-loss multiplexing~(ZALM), has been proposed. Its implementation, however, requires high-fidelity entanglement swapping across densely multiplexed time-frequency modes, which has remained an experimental challenge. Here we demonstrate entanglement swapping across 16 parallel frequency modes with a high average fidelity of 93.9$\pm$\SI{1.4}{\%}. Notably, polarization-entangled photon pairs in each frequency mode are spectrally single-mode using only off-the-shelf 50-GHz dense wavelength-division multiplexing~(DWDM) filters, eliminating the need for additional narrowband filtering. Furthermore, in order to fully exploit the temporal degree of freedom, the pump pulse is operated with a repetition frequency of \SI{3.0}{GHz}. By combining the frequency and time multiplexing, the total swapping rate reaches 5.38$\pm$0.17\,\si{pairs\,s^{-1}}, which corresponds to the ZALM Bell-pair rate of \SI{8.2e2}{pairs\,s^{-1}}. Our results establish the key experimental capabilities required for ZALM and demonstrate a scalable route toward practical high-rate quantum repeaters and long-haul quantum networks.

</details>


### [130] [An ultracompact dilution refrigerator for fast quantum device characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18699)
*Clment Geffroy,Dorian Nicolas,Eric Eyraud,Shelender Kumar,Supriya Mandal,Julien Jarreau,Laura Kowalski,Laurent Del-Rey,Didier Dufeu,Nicolas Roch,Wolfgang Wernsdorfer,Quentin Ficheux,Matias Urdampilleta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种超紧凑型稀释制冷机，能够在2.1小时内完成冷却-加热循环至70 mK，并用于高质量的超导量子器件表征，支持高通量量子硬件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀释制冷机冷却时间长、设备庞大，严重限制了超导量子器件的研发效率，亟需一种快速循环、小型化的制冷方案以加速‘制备-测量-再设计’的迭代过程。

Method: 研制了一种质量仅3 kg、直径100 mm的超紧凑型稀释制冷机，实现快速冷却-加热循环，并在搭载完整微波线路的情况下对双通量子器件进行完整表征，包括双音谱学、能量弛豫与相干时间测量及单量子比特操控基准测试。

Result: 该制冷机在无负载和有负载情况下分别可在1.2小时和2.1小时内达到70 mK，提供100 mK下20微瓦的冷却功率；尽管受限于系统基底温度，仍实现了高达99%的单量子比特门保真度。

Conclusion: 紧凑型快速循环稀释制冷机能支持当前先进水平的量子器件表征，且不牺牲测量质量，为高通量量子硬件研发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Rapid thermal cycling is a central bottleneck in the development of superconducting quantum devices: conventional dilution refrigerators require cooldowns of a day or more and substantial cryogenic infrastructure, which throttles the fabricate-measure-redesign loop. We present an ultracompact dilution refrigerator (3 kg in mass and 100 mm in diameter) that completes a full cooldown-warm-up cycle to a base temperature of 70 mK in 1.2 hours when unloaded, and in 2.1 hours when fully equipped with the microwave wiring required for qubit measurements, while delivering 20 microW of cooling power at 100 mK. We validate the platform through a complete characterization of a two-fluxonium device: we extract the full circuit Hamiltonian by two-tone spectroscopy, measure energy-relaxation and coherence times, and benchmark single-qubit control. Although the relaxation time is limited by the base temperature of the system, we reach a single-qubit gate fidelity of up to 99%, at the coherence limit set by our operating temperature. These results demonstrate that compact, fast-cycling dilution refrigeration can support state-of-the-art quantum-device characterization without sacrificing measurement quality, offering a practical route to high-throughput quantum-hardware development.

</details>


### [131] [Robustness of spin state superpositions for noisy quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18757)
*Trinidad B. Lantaño,Gabriela Wójtowicz,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在空间相关去相位噪声环境下，量子费舍信息（QFI）对相位估计精度极限的动力学影响，提出了灵敏度与退化指标，并揭示了编码-噪声结构中固有的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声环境中，量子资源容易因退相干而退化，限制了量子计量的精度，因此需要理解噪声下QFI的动力学行为以提升实际应用中的测量性能。

Method: 采用微扰理论推导出短时间弱噪声下的解析表达式，分析集体自旋矩如何影响无噪声灵敏度及噪声导致的退化，并针对高斯自旋态叠加态（如SCS、Dicke态、自旋压缩态和GHZ样态）进行QFI动力学分析。

Result: 得到了描述QFI动态演化的敏感性和退化指标的解析公式，识别出编码与噪声通勤结构中的内在权衡，并计算了最优参数和探测时间下的量子Cramér-Rao界与测量特定灵敏度界。

Conclusion: 通过提出的指标可有效刻画噪声环境下量子计量性能的演化，为设计抗噪量子传感器提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Quantum metrology faces major challenges in noisy environments, where decoherence rapidly degrades useful quantum resources. We investigate the dynamics of the precision limits given by the quantum Fisher information (QFI) for phase estimation under spatially correlated dephasing. We characterize the dynamics of the QFI by the sensitivity and degradation indicators that can be obtained as analytical expressions derived using perturbative theory treatment. These short-time and weak-noise formulas yield analytic insight into how collective-spin moments govern both (i) the noiseless sensitivity and (ii) the leading noise-induced degradation of metrological usefulness. We identify a trade-off that is intrinsic to our commuting encoding-noise structure. We analyze the QFI dynamics for the Gaussian spin state (GSS) superpositions, encompassing spin coherent state (SCS), Dicke state superpositions, spin-squeezed states, and GHZ-like states. Predictions from indicators of the QFI dynamics are compared to both the quantum Cramér--Rao bound and the measurement-specific sensitivity bounds for an optimal parameter and interrogation time under a finite total time resource. When possible, we analytically derive the measurement-specific sensitivity bounds for spin-projection and parity-based measurements.

</details>


### [132] [Interior skin focusing and directional mirror transfer in a graded non-Hermitian Krawtchouk network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18800)
*Y. S. Liu,X. Z. Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有相反梯度有向跃迁的有限开放Krawtchouk网络，发现其在中间不对称范围内表现出局部虚规范场变号、对角相似变换及等间距谱等特性，实现了亚扩展性的内部聚焦，并揭示了方向性响应与谱共度性的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索空间非互易性如何影响有限网络中的谱性质和动力学行为，尤其是在保持实数共度谱和解析可控动力学方面的挑战。

Method: 通过分析具有相反梯度有向跃迁的有限开放Krawtchouk链，利用对角相似变换将其转化为自旋旋转生成元2gJ_x，并研究其本征态、格林函数及无序扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 发现在一定参数范围内，系统存在变号的局部虚规范场，产生正定对角相似映射，导致偏置二项式包络；系统具有等间距谱和完美镜像反转能力，且方向性放大与衰减增益互为倒数；闭合链时出现规范不变虚通量，极限情况下达到N阶异常点；保留方向性格林函数比值的无序鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个精确可解的框架，将局域化几何、本征向量非正交性与共度谱及节点分辨的方向性响应联系起来，适用于理解非厄米系统中的聚焦现象和定向传输机制。

Abstract: Spatially graded nonreciprocity can move skin weight away from a boundary, but it does not generally preserve a real commensurate spectrum or analytically controlled dynamics. We study a finite open Krawtchouk network with oppositely graded directed hoppings. For a broad intermediate range of asymmetries, their local imaginary gauge field changes sign in the bulk, and its accumulated coordinate generates a positive diagonal similarity map whose normalized squared entries form a biased-binomial envelope. The same map converts the open chain into the spin-rotation generator \(2gJ_x\), so the focus and equally spaced spectrum follow from one grading. In this focusing regime, exact right, left, and biorthogonal eigenvectors show that the envelope width and participation number both scale as \(\sqrt N\), identifying a subextensive interior focus. In the physical node basis, spin rotation produces perfect mirror inversion with direction-selective amplification and attenuation whose gains are mutually inverse. The directional Green functions share their poles, while their residues differ by the same similarity ratio. Closing the chain exposes a gauge-invariant imaginary flux, and either exact one-way limit yields an exceptional point of order \(N\). Onsite disorder preserves the directional resolvent ratio, whereas independent hopping disorder breaks the clean analytic Krawtchouk map and degrades commensurability and transfer. The model therefore provides an exactly solvable finite-network framework linking localization geometry and eigenvector nonorthogonality to commensurate spectra and node-resolved directional response.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum Mixedness Testing with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18839)
*Jayadev Acharya,Abhilash Dharmavarapu,Yuhan Liu,Nengkun Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在单量子比特测量条件下对N-量子比特态进行混合性测试的问题，提出了接近完整的复杂度刻画，并给出了匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合性测试是量子态性质检验中的一个基本问题，但在实际单量子比特测量限制下其复杂度尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了一种新的依赖于测量的自适应单副本态认证下界框架以证明下界；设计了一种基于随机化Pauli基测量的新协议用于上界构造，并引入了对布尔超立方体上相关集中分布进行均匀性测试的新原语。

Result: 证明了在单量子比特测量下混合性测试的样本复杂度为n = \widetildeΘ\left(\sqrt{10}^N/\varepsilon^2\right)，实现了上下界的匹配。

Conclusion: 本文建立了单量子比特测量下混合性测试的近乎完整复杂度图像，所提方法为受限测量下的量子态检验提供了新工具。

Abstract: We consider a fundamental problem of \emph{mixedness testing}: Given $n$ copies of an $N$-qubit state $ρ$, determine whether $ρ= \mathbb{I}_d/d$ or $\|ρ-\mathbb{I}_d/d\|_1 \geq \varepsilon$ with high probability, where $d = 2^N$. In particular, we focus on performing this task in the practical setting of single-qubit measurements, where measurements are prepared independently on each qubit. We provide a nearly complete picture of single-qubit mixedness tesing by showing $n = \widetildeΘ\left(\sqrt{10}^N/\varepsilon^2\right)$. To establish our lower bound, we introduce a new measurement-dependent lower bound framework for adaptive single-copy state certification. For the upper bound, we present a randomized Pauli basis measurement protocol, which relies on a new primitive for computationally efficient uniformity testing of correlation-concentrated distributions on the Boolean hypercube.

</details>


### [134] [Copy-Protection with Correlated Challenges: Point Functions and More via Decisional Coset Monogamy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18841)
*Amit Behera,Alper Çakan,Vipul Goyal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对单解密者加密（SDE）和通用功能的新型相关挑战安全性定义，并在标准模型中基于iO和LWE假设实现了具有更强安全性的构造，解决了多个公开问题，包括点函数和计算-比较程序的纯模型复制保护及相同挑战下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复制保护方案大多仅支持独立采样挑战，无法应对更自然且重要的相同挑战场景，限制了其在实际中的应用。本文旨在加强安全定义并解决该领域多个开放问题。

Method: 引入相关挑战安全性和相关挑战不可克隆可穿孔混淆（UPO），允许挑战点之间存在任意相关性，并结合辅助信息进行安全性分析；基于iO、单向函数和量子难解LWE假设，在标准模型中构建方案。

Result: 证明Kitagawa和Yamakawa（TCC'25）的SDE构造满足新提出的相关挑战安全；在后量子iO与量子难解LWE下构造了多项式大小密钥电路的相关UPO；首次实现点函数、k-点函数、compute-and-compare程序的纯模型复制保护，以及通用可穿孔功能的相同挑战复制保护。

Conclusion: 本文通过强化安全模型并提出新的构造方法，显著推进了复制保护与单解密者加密的理论研究，为实现更实用的量子拷贝防护机制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Copy-protection encodes a functionality in a reusable quantum state that cannot be split into two states (freeloader adversaries) which remain simultaneously useful. Prior plain-model results handle only independently sampled challenges; the more natural identical-challenge notion, also tied to unclonable bits and copy-protection of point functions, has remained open. We strengthen these definitions and prove plain-model security for our new stronger notions. For single-decryptor encryption (SDE) we define correlated challenge security, show it implies all previous SDE notions including identical-challenge security, and prove that the construction of Kitagawa and Yamakawa (TCC'25) achieves it assuming iO and one-way functions. We also nearly fully characterize the relations among prior SDE notions. For general functionalities we define correlated challenge unclonable puncturable obfuscation (UPO), allowing arbitrary correlations among challenge points and puncturing bits plus auxiliary information before and after splitting, and requiring only conditionally uniform bits and $λ^c$ average conditional min-entropy in each point separately (thus, in particular, the points may be identical). Assuming post-quantum iO and quantum-hard LWE, we construct correlated UPO for polynomial-size keyed circuits with input length at least $λ^c$, answering an open question of Ananth, Behera, Huang, Kitagawa, Yamakawa (EUROCRYPT'26) and of Cakan-Goyal (EUROCRYPT'26). We also obtain the first plain-model copy protection for point functions, $k$-point functions, and compute-and-compare programs, and identical-challenge copy protection for general puncturable functionalities.

</details>


### [135] [Secure Medical Data Transmission Using Quantum Key Distribution and Post-Quantum Cryptography in Real-World Fiber Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18869)
*Vasile-Laurentiu Dosan,Paul Spooren,Sebastian Moeckel,Alessandro Zannotti,Alek Lagarrigue,Pablo Vazquez,Marc Bodenstein,Jonas Jelonek,Jansen Dwan,Fabian Steinlechner,Kevin Füchsel,Thomas Hühn,Oliver de Vries*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究展示了在德国图林根州实地部署的量子安全网络，结合了基于纠缠的量子密钥分发（QKD）与后量子密码学（PQC），在140公里实际光纤上实现了从农村医疗站到大学医院的安全通信。系统将QKD密钥直接注入标准Linux VPN隧道，并利用PQC实现端到端认证，兼容现有基础设施。系统连续自主运行22天，验证了量子安全技术在现实环境中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对量子计算机对经典公钥密码系统的威胁，推动关键基础设施（如医疗、金融、能源）向量子安全通信过渡。

Method: 采用基于纠缠的QKD与PQC相结合的分层架构，在真实部署的混合地下和架空光纤链路（共140公里）上构建可信节点网络；生成810 nm与1550 nm偏振纠缠光子对，将 telecom 波段光子送入光纤，并实施主动偏振稳定与色散补偿；QKD密钥直接注入Linux标准VPN隧道，PQC用于端到端身份认证。

Result: 实现了22天连续、完全自主的运行；成功在未改动现有医疗系统的情况下保护了远程医疗概念验证通信；架空光纤链路表现出更强的不稳定性，量子比特误码率（QBER）变化与风速高度相关。

Conclusion: 基于纠缠的QKD与PQC可有效融合于现有通信基础设施，形成实用化的量子安全网络架构，具备在真实环境中长期稳定运行的能力，为医疗等关键领域提供了可行的量子安全解决方案。

Abstract: The threat quantum computers pose to classical public-key cryptography motivates the deployment of quantum-safe communication for critical infrastructure such as healthcare, finance, and energy systems. Quantum key distribution (QKD) and post-quantum cryptography (PQC) offer complementary security guarantees, information-theoretic key exchange and quantum-resistant end-to-end authentication that can be combined in a layered architecture. Here, we demonstrate a field-deployed quantum-secure network integrating entanglement-based QKD with end-to-end PQC over 140 km of installed fiber in Thuringia, Germany, connecting a rural health kiosk to a university hospital via a trusted-node architecture comprising heterogeneous underground and aerial fiber links. Unlike conventional deployments that rely on a dedicated key management system to forward keys to applications, our architecture injects QKD keys directly into standard Linux-based VPN tunnels between adjacent nodes, while PQC secures the communication end-to-end, remaining fully compatible with existing infrastructure and software. Polarization-entangled photon pairs were generated at 810 nm and 1550 nm, with the telecom photon transmitted over deployed fiber. Active polarization stabilization and dispersion compensation preserve the entanglement and enable 22 days of continuous, fully autonomous operation, further underscoring the technological maturity of entanglement-based QKD approaches in a real-world fiber environment. Although the two deployed links were operated during separate rather than concurrent periods, the predominantly aerial link exhibited markedly greater instability, with QBER variations most strongly correlated with wind speed. The generated keys secured a telemedicine proof-of-concept without modifying existing medical systems, demonstrating a practical framework for quantum-safe critical infrastructures.

</details>


### [136] [The exact price of local realism in CHSH experiments: a measurement-dependence-detection trade-off surface, a moiré phase-locking mechanism that saturates it, and an unmeasured fringe in the fourfold coincidence sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18886)
*Aaron Alai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了CHSH实验中符合局部隐变量模型的最小测量依赖性，提出了在给定探测效率下实现特定CHSH值所需的测量依赖性下限，并证明了量子点的精确性，同时提出了一种可检验的局部机制——莫尔相位锁定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解在保持观测单率、符合率和无偏边缘分布的前提下，局部隐变量模型需要多大程度的测量依赖性才能复现CHSH实验结果。

Method: 通过在线性规划框架下优化所有局部策略，计算不同CHSH值和有效探测效率下的最小测量依赖性，并分析确定性符号模型的检测轮廓及相位锁定机制。

Result: 得到了测量依赖性M(S, η_eff)的精确表达式，验证了量子点M(2√2, 9/10)的精确值，发现了四重符合计数中cos4(a-b)调制的存在及其上限，并提出可达到理论下限的莫尔相位锁定机制。

Conclusion: 局部隐变量模型若要复现CHSH实验需显著的测量依赖性，且存在可观测的与量子预测不同的特征信号，为检验非局域性提供了新路径。

Abstract: For local hidden-variable accounts of the CHSH experiment that are faithful -- reproducing the observed singles and coincidence rates and unbiased marginals of a polarization singlet with symmetric detector efficiency $η_{\rm eff}$ -- I determine the minimal measurement dependence $M$ (Hall's variational measure) needed to achieve a CHSH value $S$. Linear programming over all local strategies yields, to machine precision at 32 grid points, $M(S,η_{\rm eff})=\max\{0,η_{\rm eff}((S+2)η_{\rm eff}-4)/6\}$, whose edges reproduce Hall's tight bound at $η_{\rm eff}=1$, the Garg-Mermin detection threshold, and the postselection ceiling $S=4/η_{\rm eff}-2$. The quantum point is certified exactly: $M(2\sqrt{2},9/10)=(27\sqrt{2}-33)/100$, with primal and dual certificates in $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{2})$ arithmetic. I solve the unique detection profile $D(m)=\sqrt{m}\,h(m)$ under which a deterministic sign model reproduces the singlet exactly, derive the $\sqrt{m}$ edge law, and prove exact quantum correlations and angle-independent coincidence rates jointly impossible for any pure-detection model of this class. Surviving local accounts trade off measurement dependence against a $\cos 4(a-b)$ modulation of the fourfold coincidence sum, of relative amplitude up to 12.4%, which vanishes identically at the CHSH angles and appears never to have been bounded below 1%. A settings-torus protocol reaches $5σ$ sensitivity at 0.1% within hours: a flat result forces $M\gtrsim 95\%$ of Hall's floor; a fringe would contradict the flat-rate prediction of quantum mechanics. Finally I exhibit a local mechanism -- moire phase locking -- with deterministic phase evolution and all randomness quenched in frozen lattice offsets and flight times, which attains the certified floor exactly at $η_{\rm eff}=1$ (10% uniform fidelity); its softening of the correlation extremes is a falsifiable fingerprint.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum Tensor Network Learning with DMRG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18901)
*Gustav J L Jäger,Martin B Plenio,Hans-Martin Rieser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将矩阵乘积态（MPS）用于机器学习，并引入全局归一化条件使其表示量子态，比较了基于梯度下降和改进DMRG的两种优化方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地将张量网络应用于机器学习任务，需要确保MPS能够有效表示量子态，并探索更优的优化方法。

Method: 引入全局归一化条件，采用梯度下降和改进的DMRG方法进行局部最优张量优化。

Result: 比较了两种优化方法在寻找局部最优张量方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 改进的DMRG方法在某些情况下比梯度下降更有效，为张量网络在机器学习中的应用提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Tensor Networks are a relatively new machine learning approach. The architectures proposed initially are inspired by approaches from quantum many-body physics simulations. One common layout is the matrix product state (MPS) also known as a tensor train optimized with gradient descent techniques. We introduce a global normalization condition, so that the MPS represents a quantum state. We investigate two optimization methods that find the locally optimal tensors and compare them regarding their effectiveness. One is based on gradient descent and the other on an adaptation of DMRG.

</details>


### [138] [Real Classical Shadows with Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18935)
*Atharva Hingane,Dax Enshan Koh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在已知噪声通道的情况下，真实经典阴影协议的完整理论，证明了即使在噪声环境下，无噪声时的采样复杂性优势依然保持，并且通过精确方差分析揭示了可观测量和噪声对性能增益的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于实际硬件无法实现理想演化，因此需要建立一个适用于包含噪声情况下的真实经典阴影协议的完整理论框架。

Method: 基于Koh和Grewal的噪声经典阴影框架，利用正交群的Weingarten微积分从第一性原理推导出噪声下的全局和局域正交阴影通道，并得出单次估计方差的闭式表达式及相关界限。

Result: 推导出了噪声下真实经典阴影协议的精确单次方差、阴影半范数及双边界，证明了无噪声时的采样复杂性优势在噪声下仍然保留；发现方差比受单一无量纲参数控制，在特定条件下可达到因子2的优势（但对秩一目标不可达），而局域实Pauli优势仍为(3/2)^k。

Conclusion: 真实经典阴影协议在噪声环境下依然优于原始方案，其性能增益具有鲁棒性，且可通过闭式准则判断何时能达到理论极限优势。

Abstract: The real classical shadows protocol of West et al. replaces the unitary (Clifford) ensemble of the Huang--Kueng--Preskill scheme by the orthogonal (real Clifford) ensemble, and for symmetric observables achieves strictly smaller estimator variances: a factor approaching two for global evolution and an exponential factor $(3/2)^k$ for $k$-local real Pauli observables. Real hardware, however, never implements the ideal evolution. Building on the noisy classical shadows framework of Koh and Grewal, we give a complete theory of the real classical shadows protocol in the presence of a known completely positive trace-preserving noise channel acting after the orthogonal evolution. We derive the noisy global and local orthogonal shadow channels from first principles using the Weingarten calculus of the orthogonal group, prove that each is a depolarizing channel acting on the symmetric (respectively locally symmetric) component of its input, and derive from it the exact single-shot variance in closed form, together with the associated shadow seminorm, two-sided bounds on it, and the resulting sample-complexity guarantees. We prove that the noiseless sample-complexity advantages survive intact under noise. Because the variances are exact rather than bounded, the ratio is controlled by a single dimensionless parameter, which gives a closed-form criterion for when the factor of two is attainable: both the second-moment and the variance ratio reach it exactly when the observable's norm profile grows, and the noise enters that limit only through a factor lying within $2/(d+2)$ of two, so the advantage is uniform in the noise. For rank-one targets it is provably unattainable, saturating strictly below two. The local real-Pauli advantage remains $(3/2)^k$. We treat complex measurement bases through a reality parameter and a transposed-noise scalar, recovering unitary shadows in the appropriate limit.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum-Enhanced Atomic Sensor via Spin Nonequilibrium Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18941)
*Ding Huang,Minwei Shi,Guzhi Bao,Keye Zhang,Weiping Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非平衡临界动力学的无噪声量子传感协议，在原子磁力计中实现了超越标准量子极限3.3 dB的测量增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子极限限制了量子传感器的灵敏度，传统突破方法依赖脆弱的非经典资源，而量子临界性虽有潜力但受限于噪声放大问题。

Method: 通过构建光驱动原子系综的非平衡动力学，在接近动态临界点时实现信号敏感度发散与量子噪声周期性回落，利用‘噪声退却’效应实现无噪声放大。

Result: 在原子磁力计中实现了3.3 dB超越标准量子极限的计量增益，并展示了对探测损耗等实验缺陷的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 非平衡临界动力学为超越量子传感基本极限提供了一个实用且通用的新范式。

Abstract: The sensitivity of quantum sensors is fundamentally constrained by the standard quantum limit (SQL) arising from intrinsic quantum fluctuations. While non-classical resources like squeezing or entanglement can surpass this limit, their utility is often restricted by the extreme fragility of entangled states and the complexity of their preparation. Quantum criticality offers a compelling alternative by harnessing divergent susceptibility to amplify signals without requiring fragile non-classical resources. However, the practical benefit of this approach has remained controversial due to the potential for the simultaneous amplification of quantum noise. Here, we demonstrate a universal protocol for noiseless critical sensing by engineering a light-driven atomic ensemble near a dynamical critical point. Analogous to a Kapitza pendulum near its inverted orientation, the spin system enters a non-equilibrium regime where the signal susceptibility diverges while the quantum noise periodically recedes to its coherent baseline. We exploit this ``noise ebbing'' to create a built-in noiseless amplifier, demonstrating a 3.3 dB metrological gain over the SQL in an atomic magnetometer. Our implementation exhibits intrinsic robustness against common experimental imperfections such as detection losses, establishing non-equilibrium critical dynamics as a practical and versatile paradigm for surpassing the fundamental limits of quantum sensing.

</details>


### [140] [AlphaClifford: Efficient Clifford Synthesis and Transpilation with Model-based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18946)
*Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Jacopo Cossio,Carla Piazza,Giuseppe Serra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaClifford，一种基于模型的强化学习框架，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索，用于高效合成由H、S和CNOT门构成的Clifford电路，在降低门数量方面优于现有方法，并可扩展应用于硬件约束下的量子线路优化和Clifford+T逻辑合成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Clifford电路综合方法（如Aaronson-Gottesman算法）通常产生门数过多的次优电路，缺乏对电路代价的有效优化，限制了其在量子纠错和容错计算中的效率。

Method: 提出AlphaClifford框架，利用强化学习与蒙特卡洛树搜索，结合辛群的代数性质建模状态空间，在组合空间中有效搜索最优Clifford电路结构，以最小化总门数和两量子比特门（CNOT）数量。

Result: 在无约束Clifford优化中，相比现有启发式方法显著减少总门数和CNOT门数；在硬件约束下的线路转译任务中优于现有基于RL的编译器；并可作为Clifford+T合成流程中的后处理模块有效优化结果。

Conclusion: 基于模型的强化学习能有效应对量子编译中的组合复杂性，AlphaClifford为当前及未来的容错量子设备提供了一条可扩展的路径以缓解硬件限制。

Abstract: Clifford circuits play a foundational role in quantum computing, particularly due to their importance in quantum error correction and fault-tolerant logical synthesis. While these circuits can be efficiently simulated and represented as symplectic matrices, standard synthesis methods-such as the Aaronson-Gottesman algorithm-often yield sub-optimal circuits with excessively high gate counts. In this work, we introduce AlphaClifford, a model-based Reinforcement Learning framework powered by Monte Carlo Tree Search, designed to efficiently synthesize Clifford circuits from the fundamental gate set composed of H, S, and CNOT. By modeling the state space through the algebraic properties of the symplectic group, AlphaClifford effectively explores this combinatorial space to minimize overall circuit cost. For unconstrained Clifford optimization, our approach achieves a consistent reduction in both total and two-qubit (CNOT) gate counts compared to state-of-the-art synthesis heuristics, despite operating with a strictly less expressive gate set. Furthermore, we demonstrate the broad applicability of our framework on two additional tasks: hardware-constrained Clifford transpilation, where we outperform existing RL-based compilers, and as a post-synthesis optimization component within a full Clifford+T logical synthesis pipeline. Our results underscore that model-based RL is highly effective at addressing the combinatorial complexities of quantum compilation, offering a scalable pathway to mitigate hardware constraints in both near-term and future fault-tolerant quantum devices.

</details>


### [141] [An ultra-bright, highly-scalable, squeezed light source for hybrid quantum photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18950)
*Kai-Hong Luo,Denis Kopylov,Florian Lütkewitte,Jan-Lucas Eickmann,Simone Atzeni,Fabian Schlue,Benjamin Brecht,Torsten Meier,Polina Sharapova,Michael Stefszky,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种满足混合量子光子学多重需求的单通、周期极化Type-II KTP波导光源，具备高亮度、单模性好、与光纤网络兼容等优点，并建立了完整的理论框架用于基于光子计数的光源表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服传统光子计数方案中模式不敏感带来的挑战，并实现连续变量与离散变量量子光学优势的融合，需要开发一种兼具单模性、可扩展性、强压缩能力和兼容现有探测与传输技术的光源。

Method: 采用单通、周期极化的Type-II KTP波导生成单模压缩真空态，并通过测量有效模式数、光子产率和波长特性评估性能；同时构建完整理论框架以分析高增益和损耗下的复杂动力学。

Result: 实现了有效模式数为1.24的高质量单模压缩态，亮度高达每脉冲40,000个光子，中心波长1546 nm，支持光纤传输和超导纳米线探测器中的光子数分辨。

Conclusion: 该光源是目前最理想的混合量子光子学光源之一，结合实验优化与理论建模，为大规模集成量子光子系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum photonics seeks to combine the complementary advantages of continuous- and discrete-variable quantum optics. This typically entails photon-counting measurements on entangled states generated by interfering many single-mode squeezed-vacuum (SMSV) states. However, because conventional photon-counting schemes are mode-insensitive, it is critical that the SMSV states occupy a single, well-defined mode. Achieving this requires careful engineering of the process, which determines both the spatial and spectro-temporal properties of the generated state. In addition, the ideal source must be massively scalable, capable of efficiently generating strong squeezing, and remain compatible with existing detection schemes and fiber networks. Although many platforms address one or more of these requirements, satisfying all of them simultaneously remains challenging.
  Here, we present a source that meets all of these requirements: a single-pass, periodically poled, Type-II potassium titanyl phosphate (KTP) waveguide optimized for scalable hybrid quantum-photonic architectures. The SMSV state produced by the source has a measured effective mode number of 1.24. Furthermore, the source is extremely bright (producing up to 40 000 photons per pulse) and operates at a central wavelength of 1546nm, optimized for fiber-network compatibility and which, in combination with picosecond duration, also enables intrinsic photon-number resolution in superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors. Although this source constitutes an ideal source in a simplified picture, the ultimate limitations of any source will be governed by complex dynamics that arise when the system is driven at high-gain or due to unavoidable loss during state generation. We have therefore developed a complete theoretical framework that enables a comprehensive photon-counting-based characterization of the source.

</details>


### [142] [Deviation from linear reduced dynamics always occurs for each non-factorisable system-environment unitary evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18977)
*Iman Sargolzahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子系统与其环境相互作用下的约化动力学的线性性条件，指出当联合系统的幺正演化不可分解时，为获得线性约化动力学，初始态集合必须是全体密度算符的真子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在一般情况下量子系统约化动力学是否为线性映射，并分析其成立条件，特别是幺正演化结构和初态集合的影响。

Method: 通过分析系统-环境联合初态集合 \(\mathcal{S}\) 与幺正演化 \(U\) 的关系，讨论约化动力学的线性性，特别区分因子化与非因子化 \(U\) 的情形。

Result: 证明当 \(U\) 可分解时，约化动力学对任意初态都是线性的；而对任何不可分解的 \(U\)，必须限制初态集合为 \(\mathcal{D}\) 的真子集才能保证线性性。此外，在特定凸初态集下，若某一 \(U_1\) 下动力学为正但非完全正，则存在另一 \(U_2\) 下动力学非线性。

Conclusion: 非因子化的幺正演化下无法普遍实现线性约化动力学，线性性依赖于对初态集合的限制。

Abstract: In the simplest approximation, the reduced dynamics of a quantum system $S$ interacting with its environment $E$ is considered to be given by a completely positive map. But, in general, this is not the case. In fact, the reduced dynamics of the system in not even linear, in general. Whether the reduced dynamics is linear or not is determined by two factors: the set of possible initial states of the system-environment $\mathcal{S}=\left\lbrace ρ_{SE} \right\rbrace $, and the joint system-environment unitary evolution $U$. When $U$ is factorisable as $U=U_S\otimes U_E$, then we can choose $\mathcal{S}=\mathcal{D}$, where $\mathcal{D}$ is the set of all system-environment density operators. In other words, when $U$ is factorisable, the reduced dynamics of the system $S$ is linear (in fact unitary) for arbitrary initial state of the system-environment $ρ_{SE}$. We show that this result cannot be generalized to any non-factorisable $U$: For any non-factorisable unitary evolution of the whole system-environment $U$, the set $\mathcal{S}$ must be chosen as a proper subset of $\mathcal{D}$ to achieve linear reduced dynamics. As a byproduct, considering a convex set of possible initial states of the system-environment $\mathcal{S}$ such that $\mathrm{Tr}_{E} \ \mathcal{S}=\mathrm{Tr}_{E} \ \mathcal{D}$, we show that when the reduced dynamics of the system, for one system-environment unitary evolution $U_1$, is positive, but not completely positive, this implies that reduced dynamics is not linear for another $U_2$.

</details>


### [143] [RushHour: A Dynamically Reconfigurable Lattice-Surgery Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18985)
*Nathaniel Tornow,Aleksandra Świerkowska,Peter Wegmann,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RushHour的动态格子手术（dynamic lattice surgery）方法，通过硬件-编译器协同设计实现量子计算中资源受限下的高效容错操作，显著提升了在小型和空间受限量子芯片上的执行效率和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的格子手术方法过于僵化，预先分配资源导致无法适应小规模芯片、存在较大的时间开销，并且局限于特定的空间-时间权衡点，难以满足实际容错量子计算的需求。

Method: 提出动态格子手术技术，支持辅助空间的动态重构、资源态的即时分配和逻辑量子比特的动态旋转；通过RushHour指令集架构（ISA）、格子管理单元（LMU）和专用编译器实现硬件与软件的协同优化。

Result: 在最小的量子芯片上，86%的基准测试仅能由RushHour运行，现有方法需要大1.2-3.5倍的芯片；在空间受限的早期容错量子芯片上，RushHour比最佳可行方案快2.0-7.2倍，在大型芯片上性能与现有最优水平相当；其实现结果达到理想机器资源极限的4.8倍效率。

Conclusion: RushHour通过统一的动态格子手术框架，实现了全范围空间-时间权衡的覆盖，显著提升了资源利用率和执行效率，为资源受限环境下的实用化容错量子计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Practical fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) requires efficient lattice surgery (LS), so that large algorithms fit on resource-constrained quantum chips. Existing approaches, however, are rigid: qubits, routing space, and resource states are allocated ahead of execution, which prevents running on small chips, leaves statically scheduled executions with large time overheads, and fixes each design at a single area of the space-time trade-off.
  We present dynamic LS, which enables efficient reconfiguration of the ancilla space, just-in-time allocation of resource states, and dynamic rotations of logical qubits, thereby spanning the entire space-time trade-off with a single, unified approach. We realize dynamic LS with RushHour through a hardware-compiler co-design: the RushHour ISA formalizes and programs our dynamic lattice model, the Lattice Management Unit abstracts dynamic lattice management and performs efficient lattice reconfiguration, and the RushHour Compiler compiles logical circuits for physical chips into optimized ISA programs while pipelining instructions.
  We evaluate RushHour against six state-of-the-art compilers and two resource models. On the smallest chips, 86% of benchmarks run only with RushHour, while existing approaches require 1.2-3.5$\times$ larger chips. On space-constrained early-FTQC chips, RushHour runs a median 2.0-7.2$\times$ faster than the best feasible alternative, while achieving results comparable to the state of the art on very large chips. RushHour's constructive results run 4.8$\times$ from an idealized-machine resource limit.

</details>


### [144] [Bernstein-Vazirani Networks: Quantum Machine Learning by Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19043)
*Natacha Kuete Meli,Tolga Birdal,Prayag Tiwari,Vladislav Golyanik,Michael Moeller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种非变分量子机器学习框架Bernstein-Vazirani网络（BVNs），利用量子干涉进行监督学习，在图像和表示学习任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种无需梯度训练、具有强泛化能力的量子机器学习模型，克服传统变分量子算法训练困难的问题。

Method: 基于量子傅里叶采样原理，将标签数据置于叠加态并在傅里叶基下干涉以提取全局特征；进一步提出广义BVNs，支持在问题适配基下的干涉，提升模型表达能力。

Result: 在合成与真实分类任务及隐式图像表示中，BVNs展现出强泛化能力和与经典及量子基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: BVNs是一种无需梯度训练、具备通用函数逼近能力的量子机器学习框架，通过过完备干涉基实现高效特征提取。

Abstract: We introduce Bernstein-Vazirani Networks (BVNs), a non-variational quantum machine learning framework that leverages quantum interference for supervised learning, demonstrated on vision and representation learning tasks. In their standard form, BVNs follow the principle of quantum Fourier sampling: labelled data are placed in superposition and interfered in the Fourier basis to extract globally informative features. We then define generalised BVNs that enable interference in problem-adapted bases, yielding more expressive models under the same measurement budget as in the standard setting. BVNs achieve universal function approximation through (over)complete interference bases, while training of BVNs is gradient-free. Experiments on synthetic and real-world classification tasks, as well as implicit image representation, show strong generalisation capabilities and competitive performance with classical and quantum baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Non-Local Search-to-Decision Reduction over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19091)
*Prabhanjan Ananth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非局域搜索与决策问题，证明了当两个不通信的方通过局部测量恢复随机字符串x的概率为p时，他们正确回答相同奇偶性挑战的概率至多为min{1, 1/2 + 5p^(1/22)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受不可克隆加密和量子复制保护等应用驱动，旨在理解非局域条件下信息提取的极限。

Method: 采用信息论方法进行证明，未提供高效的提取器，且证明过程借助了人工智能工具辅助完成。

Result: 得到了双方在局部测量下恢复x的概率p与共同正确预测随机奇偶性的概率之间的上界关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在非局域设置中，同时预测相同奇偶性任务的成功概率受限于局部恢复原始数据的能力。

Abstract: Non-local search-to-decision asks whether two noncommunicating parties, given the two shares of a bipartite encoding of a uniformly random string $x\in \mathbb{F}_2^n$, can both predict the same random parity $\langle r,x\rangle$ without there also being local measurements with which both parties recover $x$. We prove that if their optimal probability of both recovering $x$ by local measurements is $p$, then their probability of both answering a common parity challenge correctly is at most $\min\{1,\frac{1}{2}+5p^{1/22}\}$. The result is motivated by applications to unclonable encryption and quantum copy-protection. The proof is information-theoretic and does not provide an efficient extractor. The proof and the exposition were developed with assistance from ChatGPT using GPT-5.6 Sol Pro and Codex in the Ultra reasoning mode.

</details>


### [146] [Realizing Logical Diagonal Gates via Transversal Physical $Z$-Rotations in CSS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19094)
*K. Sai Mineesh Reddy,Navin Kashyap*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“附加构造”方法，通过系统地向现有CSS量子码添加物理比特，使其能够通过横向Z旋转实现目标逻辑Z旋转门，从而支持容错逻辑门操作，代价是增加物理比特开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子计算需要容错的逻辑门，而传统CSS码主要优化码参数，缺乏对逻辑门实现能力的关注。因此，需要一种方法来构造支持特定逻辑Z旋转门的CSS码。

Method: 通过分析实现逻辑对角门的CSS码嵌套经典码对(C1, C2)的条件，提出“附加构造”方法：在原有CSS码基础上添加新的物理比特，并通过对这些新增比特施加适当的横向Z旋转来实现目标逻辑门。

Result: 证明了仅能通过横向Z旋转实现单量子比特Z旋转和多控制Z旋转；提出了可扩展的附加构造方法，能将任意CSS码扩展以支持多个所需逻辑Z旋转门，且可通过参数选择控制最小距离损失。

Conclusion: 附加构造为构建支持容错逻辑Z旋转门的CSS码提供了一种系统化方法，虽带来额外物理比特开销，但实现了逻辑门与编码方案的灵活结合，适用于需多种逻辑门的量子计算场景。

Abstract: Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes, constructed from nested classical codes $C_2 \subseteq C_1$, are typically optimized for good code parameters. However, practical quantum computing equally demands fault-tolerant logical gates. In this work, we characterize nested pairs $(C_1, C_2)$ whose resulting CSS codes realize a target logical diagonal gate via transversal physical $Z$-rotations. In doing so, we recover a result of Camps-Moreno et al. that CSS codes can realize only logical single-qubit $Z$-rotations and multi-qubit controlled-$Z$ rotations via transversal physical $Z$-rotations. Building on our characterization, we develop the ''appending construction'', that takes as input an $[[n',k']]$ CSS code $Q'$ and a target logical $Z$-rotation (single-qubit or multi-controlled) $U_L$, and extends $Q'$ by systematically appending $n''$ physical qubits to obtain an $[[n,k]]$ CSS code $Q$ with $n = n'+n''$ and $k=k'$. The target logical gate $U_L$ is realized in $Q$ by applying a well-chosen physical transversal $Z$-rotation to the $n''$ appended physical qubits. The CSS code $Q$ may incur a loss in minimum distance, but the loss can be controlled through the parameter choices made in the construction. By repeatedly applying the appending construction, we can extend any CSS code $Q'$ to obtain a CSS code $Q$ that supports fault-tolerant implementations of multiple desired logical $Z$-rotations. The cost to be paid for this is the increased physical qubit overhead as the number of target logical gates grows.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum circuit optimization using deep reinforcement learning: Applications across multiple gate sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19103)
*Khoa Dang Tao,Sumin Jin,Muhammad Raza,Changhyoup Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种将确定性规则嵌入强化学习环境的量子电路优化框架（RL+CR），使代理能够专注于非平凡优化，显著提升电路压缩效果，并展现出良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的量子电路优化方法在训练过程中大量时间用于重新发现已被确定性方法解决的基本简化规则，效率低下，因此需要一种能结合两者优势的新框架。

Method: 设计了一个强化学习框架，将确定性的交换与约简（CR）算法嵌入到训练环境中；每次代理执行动作后，CR算法自动处理基本的门交换和抵消，从而让代理聚焦于更复杂的优化任务。

Result: 在Clifford+T和CNOT+Pauli两种门集上实验表明，RL+CR在所有测试规模上均优于标准RL代理；在20量子比特、比训练电路大五倍的Clifford+T电路上，RL+CR移除的门数量是标准RL的两倍。

Conclusion: 该方法通过融合确定性规则与强化学习，有效提升了量子电路优化的效率与可扩展性，有望加速未来容错及实用规模量子系统的编译过程。

Abstract: The practical implementation of quantum algorithms on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices encounters operational limitations due to decoherence and other sources of noise inherent in real hardware. To mitigate these errors while preserving the original functionality of the algorithm, shorter quantum circuits are therefore preferred. This motivates the development of effective quantum circuit optimization algorithms. Learning-based approaches have emerged as a leading candidate, yet existing autonomous agents remain inefficient, spending most of their training capacity rediscovering elementary reductions that deterministic rule-based methods already handle reliably. To address this challenge, we propose a reinforcement learning framework that embeds a deterministic Commutation-and-Reduction (CR) algorithm directly into the training environment. After every agent action, the CR algorithm automatically resolves elementary commutations and cancellations, enabling the agent to focus its learning capacity on the non-trivial optimizations where reinforcement learning adds real value. Empirical evaluation across two gate sets, the universal Clifford+T basis and the CNOT+Pauli basis, shows that RL+CR produces shorter circuits than a standard RL agent at all tested scales. We demonstrate that RL trained on smaller quantum circuits can be applied to larger quantum circuits. On 20-qubit Clifford+T circuits, five times larger than the training circuits, RL+CR removes twice as many gates as standard RL. This work provides a robust approach that could accelerate the compilation and optimization processes for future fault-tolerant and utility-scale quantum systems.

</details>


### [148] [Connectivity--Interference Competition in Coherent Transport on Percolated Hierarchical Small-World Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19130)
*Miquéias Jacinto Cirino,Marcos César de Oliveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在分层小世界网络中，相干量子输运表现出非单调行为：随着网络连接度增加，输运效率先升高后降低，出现“过度连接惩罚”现象，表明完全连接并非最优，相干干涉和谱结构重组是其物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相干量子输运在复杂网络中的行为，探索与经典输运相反的现象及其物理根源。

Method: 采用连续时间量子行走模型，在渗流的分层小世界网络上分析根节点到边界层的输运效率，结合谱分析和与经典及退相输运的对比。

Result: 发现相干量子输运存在“过度连接惩罚”——输运效率在中等连接度时最大，并随完全连接而下降；该现象源于捷径促进扩散与干涉引起的层内循环之间的竞争，且由谱简并和本征态重排驱动。

Conclusion: 相干量子输运的最优性能出现在中间连接密度，完全连接反而不利；这为无序光子系统和量子网络架构设计提供了新的原理。

Abstract: Adding links generally improves classical transport by increasing the number of available paths. We show that coherent quantum transport can display the opposite behavior. Using continuous-time quantum walks on a percolated hierarchical small-world network, we identify a coherent overconnectivity penalty: root-to-boundary transport is maximized at intermediate bond probability and decreases as the network approaches full connectivity. The effect is quantified by the final-layer limiting probability $χ_N$ and by the penalty $P_Q=1-χ_N(p=1)/\max_pχ_N(p)$, which measures the loss caused by making the architecture fully connected. The optimum results from a competition between shortcut-assisted spreading and interference-induced intra-layer recirculation. Spectral analysis shows that bond dilution creates motif-induced degeneracies and reorganizes the eigenstates connecting the root to the outermost layer. A comparison with dephased and classical transport shows that the non-monotonic landscape is not a purely geometrical percolation effect, but a coherent architecture-dependent phenomenon. These results provide a design principle for coherent transport in disordered photonic and quantum-network architectures.

</details>


### [149] [Entanglement battery and entanglement catalyst in local state discrimination problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19139)
*Saronath Halder,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在局域操作和经典通信下，利用纠缠电池和纠缠催化剂进行正交纯态区分的限制与优势，指出集合元素个数必须小于希尔伯特空间总维度，并构造了纠缠资源在精确与近似情形下的非平凡应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索纠缠电池和纠缠催化剂在局域状态区分中的作用，揭示其在精确与近似情形下的能力边界与潜在优势。

Method: 通过理论分析和构造具体例子，研究在LOCC下使用精确或近似纠缠电池或催化剂区分正交纯态的条件与效果。

Result: 证明完美区分需集合大小小于希尔伯特空间总维度；构造出纠缠电池带来显著优势的非平凡情况；发现近似工具在多副本不可区分系综衍生集合中特别有效。

Conclusion: 纠缠电池和催化剂在特定条件下能显著提升局域区分能力，尤其是近似工具具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we study the limitations and advantages of using entanglement battery and entanglement catalyst in local state discrimination problems. We consider both cases of such tools, i.e., exact and approximate cases. We show that to distinguish any set of orthogonal pure bipartite entangled states perfectly under local operations and classical communication using an (exact) entanglement battery or an (exact) entanglement catalyst, it is necessary to consider that the cardinality of the set must be smaller than the total dimension of the given Hilbert space. Then, we construct a nontrivial case where an exact entanglement battery can provide huge advantage. We also construct other nontrivial cases where exact or approximate entanglement battery or entanglement catalyst can be useful. In fact, we find that the approximate tools are particularly useful in the local discrimination of certain sets which can be derived from many-copy indistinguishable ensembles.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum Speedups Require Structure or Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19158)
*Guy Blanc,Jordan Docter,Carmen Strassle,Li-Yang Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子复杂性理论中的一个基本猜想，证明了对于并行量子算法，大多数输入上的t查询d轮量子算法可以用t^{O(d^2)}次经典查询模拟，表明无结构问题的超多项式加速需要超常深度的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子复杂性理论中关于量子算法能否被经典算法有效模拟的基本猜想，特别是针对并行量子算法的情形。

Method: 通过分析t查询d轮并行量子算法的结构，利用数学工具证明其可在大多数输入上用poly(t)级别的经典查询进行模拟。

Result: 证明了每个t查询d轮量子算法可以在大多数输入上通过t^{O(d^2)}次经典查询模拟，揭示了无结构问题中超多项式和指数级加速对量子电路深度的要求。

Conclusion: 该结果支持了在无结构问题中实现显著量子加速的困难性，并解释了为何多数已知的量子优势出现在具有结构的问题中，同时对BPP与BQP在随机预言机下的关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: One of the most basic conjectures in quantum complexity theory states that every $t$-query quantum algorithm can be simulated on most inputs by a $\mathrm{poly}(t)$-query classical algorithm. If true, this would provide broad justification for the need for structure in quantum speedups.
  We settle this conjecture for parallel quantum algorithms, showing that every $t$-query $d$-round quantum algorithm can be simulated on most inputs with $t^{O(d^2)}$ classical queries. This suggests that for unstructured problems, superpolynomial speedups would require quantum circuits of superconstant depth, and exponential speedups would further require polynomial depth. In contrast, most known speedups for structured problems are achieved by highly parallel, low-depth algorithms.
  Our techniques also carry new implications for the status of $\mathsf{BPP}$ vs. $\mathsf{BQP}$ relative to a random oracle, a similarly longstanding problem.

</details>


### [151] [State--Generator Geometry of Open Quantum Systems: Compatibility and Covariant Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19175)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuna*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于在开放量子系统的控制空间中传输定态响应的几何框架，通过嵌入状态与动力学生成器的有序对，构建了度量、响应一形式和闭合二形式，并展示了其在光学布洛赫模型中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在开放量子系统中实现定态响应在控制空间中的有效传输，需要建立一种内在的几何结构来描述系统响应的变化与迁移。

Method: 将物理模型表示为定态与动力学生成器的有序对，嵌入共同的环境空间以诱导出度量、响应一形式和闭合二形式；利用自动微分计算列维-奇维塔联络，并通过两点计算比较测地线与线性插值路径。

Result: 在振幅阻尼光学布洛赫模型中，度量与响应二形式仅在特定参数点兼容，其余区域物理流形为非凯勒；计算得到的测地线比控制空间中的线性插值路径更短。

Conclusion: 该几何框架无需凯勒相容性即可定义联络、测地线和平行传输，为在定态模型间传递可观测量响应（包括多维谱）提供了内在导数与传输结构。

Abstract: We develop a geometry for transporting stationary-state response across the control space of an open quantum system. A physical model is represented by the ordered pair of its stationary state and dynamical generator. Embedding these pairs in a common ambient space induces a metric, a response one-form, and a closed two-form on the control manifold. The ambient space admits a canonical complex structure that exchanges state and generator directions, but the Liouvillian null-state condition restricts physical models to a submanifold that need not preserve this structure. For an amplitude-damped optical Bloch model, the metric and response two-form are compatible at the single point $ω=0$ and $g/Γ=1/\sqrt{2}$; the physical manifold is non-Kähler elsewhere. The induced metric defines the Levi--Civita connection, geodesics, and parallel transport without requiring Kähler compatibility. We compute the connection by automatic differentiation through the stationary Liouvillian solve and recover the symbolic result to machine precision. A two-point calculation then shows that the resulting geodesic differs from linear interpolation in control space and follows a shorter path through the family of state--generator models. This geometry supplies the intrinsic derivative and transport structure needed to carry observable response, including multidimensional spectra, between admissible stationary models.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Embedding Paired Free-Fermion Gaussian States into Gutzwiller-Projected Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer Wave Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18168)
*Ryui Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种将自旋无质量费米子的高斯态精确嵌入到自旋有质量Abrikosov费米子的Gutzwiller投影BCS态中的方法，可用于非可积模型的变分蒙特卡洛研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了为非可积模型提供可控的变分蒙特卡洛初态，需要将简单的费米子高斯态嵌入到更复杂的投影BCS框架中。

Method: 以一维横向场伊辛模型为例，通过Gutzwiller投影BCS波函数，将任意偶宇称无自旋费米子高斯态表示为成对指数形式，并嵌入到自旋Abrikosov费米子系统中。

Result: 实现了将自旋无质量费米子高斯态精确嵌入到投影BCS态中，提供了适用于变分蒙特卡洛计算的有效初始态。

Conclusion: 该构造方法为研究强关联体系中的非可积模型提供了新的工具和起点。

Abstract: Gutzwiller-projected Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) wave functions of Abrikosov fermions are widely used to describe quantum many-body states. Taking the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model as an example, we exactly embed any even-parity spinless fermionic Gaussian state representable as a paired exponential in the chosen particle basis into a projected BCS state of spinful Abrikosov fermions. The construction provides controlled initial states for variational Monte Carlo studies of nonintegrable models.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Longitudinal Relational Publics and their Discursive Overlap with Issue Publics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18422)
*Alyssa Hasegawa Smith,Judith Gilsbach,Ahana Bhattacharya,Holliday Sims,Kenneth Joseph*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“纵向关系化公众”（longitudinal relational networked public）这一新构念，用于研究政治议题如何进入非政治性的在线第三空间，并通过Twitter用户面板数据案例分析其在2020年美国选举和“黑人的命也是命”运动中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解谁将政治话题带入名义上非政治的在线空间以及何时发生，需要一种能够同时捕捉发言者与听众关系并随时间保持稳定的分析框架。

Method: 基于Bruns关于个人中心型公众与议题中心型公众的区分，提出‘纵向关系化公众’概念，并通过对匹配美国选民记录的Twitter用户面板数据构建150个此类公众，分析其与选举政治和BLM议题公众的话语重叠情况。

Result: 研究发现，最符合‘第三空间’特征的在线空间拥有最具政治异质性的受众，因而具备最大的跨党派对话潜力；此外，观众的注意力模式显著影响内容创作者的话语取向。

Conclusion: 引入关注受众的关系性构念有助于揭示政治讨论如何渗透到日常在线生活中，并指出了推动跨群体政治交流的关键机制。

Abstract: Online discussions of political issues do not always happen in places explicitly dedicated to political talk; they also arise in online spaces focused on at least nominally apolitical interests, identities, and/or places. Whatever one's normative view of politics entering these ``online third spaces,'' understanding who brings political issues into them, and when, requires studying these spaces at scale. In turn, studying these spaces at scale requires a construct that captures both who is speaking and who is listening, and that holds up over time. Building on Bruns' distinction between participant-centered personal publics and post-centered issue publics, we introduce the longitudinal relational networked public (or, simply, the longitudinal public): the coupling of discourse produced by a socially connected set of creators with the durable attention their shared audience gives it. The longitudinal public departs from related relational constructs in three ways: it is anchored in the attention patterns of a non-elite, population-level audience; it treats within-public structure as an object of analysis rather than assuming homogeneity; and it incorporates the audience as a force that shapes creator discourse. In a case study, we identify 150 longitudinal publics from the following ties of a panel of Twitter/X users matched to U.S. voter records, then measure their discursive overlap with the electoral politics and Black Lives Matter issue publics across 2020, a period spanning the murder of George Floyd and the general election. We find that the spaces that best fit the idea of a third space have the most politically heterogeneous audiences, and thus the most potential room for cross-partisan talk. This result, among several others, shows how a relational, audience-aware construct can reveal where and through whom political talk enters everyday online life.

</details>


### [154] [Comparing Probabilistic Influence-Spreading Centralities to Commonly-Used Centrality Measures in Directed and Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18797)
*Juuso Luhtala,Vesa Kuikka,Kimmo K. Kaski*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于影响传播模型（ISM）的三种概率中心性度量方法，用于衡量有向加权网络中节点的影响发送、接收及中介作用，并通过真实与合成网络验证其与传统中心性指标的相关性，发现传统指标在捕捉接收影响和概率中介角色方面存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中心性指标难以准确刻画有向加权网络中节点在概率影响传播下的实际作用，尤其在影响接收和中介路径多样性方面表现不足，因此需要新的度量方法以更全面反映节点影响力。

Method: 提出影响传播模型（ISM），定义出中心性、入中心性和ISM介数中心性三种概率中心性指标，考虑边的传输概率及最长路径长度限制；在四种真实在线社交网络和九种合成网络上，与加权出度、入度、接近中心性、最短路径介数和Katz中心性等进行比较，采用皮尔逊相关系数和斯皮尔曼秩相关系数评估相似性。

Result: ISM出中心性与加权出度及外向Katz中心性在低边概率下表现出强相关性；ISM入中心性与其他指标的关系依赖于网络拓扑，有时呈现负相关；ISM介数中心性与最短路径介数的相关性随替代影响路径增多而减弱。

Conclusion: 标准中心性指标可在一定程度上近似广播式影响的传播效果，但在描述影响接收能力和概率性中介角色时往往忽略关键细节，ISM框架能更细致地揭示这些动态特性。

Abstract: The Influence-Spreading Model (ISM) introduces three probabilistic centrality measures: out-centrality, in-centrality, and ISM betweenness centrality. Out-centrality measures the average probability that a node influences others, while in-centrality measures the average probability that others influence a node. ISM betweenness centrality measures the change in total probabilistic influence when a node is removed. These measures depend on edge transmission probabilities and allow walks up to a specified maximum length. We compare the ISM centrality measures to commonly used weighted variants of out-degree, in-degree, closeness, shortest-path betweenness, and Katz centrality in directed, weighted networks using four real-world online social networks and nine synthetic networks generated by Erdős-Rényi, navigable small-world, and directed scale-free models. For the synthetic networks, the edge probabilities are drawn from three beta distributions. We evaluate the similarity in centrality values and their ranking using Pearson correlation and Spearman's rank correlation coefficients. Results show strong correlations between the ISM out-centrality and weighted out-degree and outward Katz centrality, particularly for low edge probabilities. Conversely, relationships between the ISM in-centrality and other measures vary with network topology, sometimes yielding negative correlations. Correlations between the ISM betweenness and the shortest-path betweenness are also topology-dependent and weaken as alternative influence paths become more relevant. Overall, standard centrality measures can approximate the influence of broadcasting influence but often miss the nuances of receiving influence and probabilistic intermediary roles.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Distribution-Agnostic Isocontour Confidence Bounds for Robust Uncertainty Visualization of Scalar Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18220)
*Timbwaoga A. J. Ouermi,Nina M. Gottschling,Alex Gorczowski,Tushar M. Athawale*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Hoeffding不等式的分布无关的置信带方法，用于可视化标量场中等值线的空间不确定性，在合成和真实数据集上表现出对真值的良好覆盖性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如高斯假设或自助法在样本数较少时可能低估不确定性，且依赖特定分布假设，导致置信区间误导；需要一种不依赖分布假设、稳健的不确定性可视化方法。

Method: 利用Hoeffding不等式构建顶点级置信界，并传播生成等值线置信带，该方法无需分布假设，适用于小样本集合。

Result: 在合成和真实集合数据上的实验表明，Hoeffding置信带虽较宽松，但能更准确地捕捉真实值，避免了高斯和自助法可能遗漏的问题，同时保持较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: Hoeffding置信带是一种鲁棒、分布无关的补充性技术，可有效缓解因分布假设导致的误导性不确定性估计，适用于等值线的不确定性可视化。

Abstract: Uncertainty visualization has been shown to be pivotal for conveying the reliability of features extracted from scalar fields. Features represented by isocontours, mean isocontours lack an indication of spatial uncertainty, whereas spaghetti isocontour plots can become cluttered and difficult to interpret. Existing methods relying on specific distribution assumptions, such as Gaussian and nonparametric bootstrap, to provide compact clutter-free spatial confidence bounds but may underestimate uncertainty for ensembles with a limited number of samples. We introduce a robust, distribution-agnostic Hoeffding confidence band as a novel complementary (and not competitive) technique to mitigate potentially misleading uncertainty bounds that may arise from distribution-based assumptions. The approach constructs vertex-wise confidence bounds using Hoeffding's inequality and propagates them to generate isocontour confidence bands. Results on synthetic and real ensemble datasets show that the Hoeffding confidence bands are loose but accurately capture underlying true values that may be missed by the Gaussian and bootstrap alternatives while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [156] [Multi-Objective Optimization Under Uncertainty of Part Quality in Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18429)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Paromita Nath,Matthew Sato,Sankaran Mahadevan,Paul Witherell*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯神经网络的数据驱动方法，用于熔融沉积成型过程中的多目标不确定性优化，兼顾几何精度和焊道质量，并通过实验验证了模型与优化方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在熔融沉积成型（FFF）过程中同时优化多个存在不确定性的工艺参数，以提高制造零件的几何精度和纤维结合质量。

Method: 通过实验采集零件质量数据，构建贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）模型来预测几何失真和结合质量，并考虑模型和输入的不确定性；采用鲁棒性优化方法进行多目标优化，获得帕累托前沿。

Result: 成功建立了能捕捉认知和随机不确定性的BNN预测模型，优化后可有效权衡几何精度与结合质量，不同多目标场景下的帕累托面被构建并经实际制造验证。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效处理FFF过程中的不确定性，在多目标优化中实现稳健设计，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This work presents a data-driven methodology for multi-objective optimization under uncertainty of process parameters in the fused filament fabrication (FFF) process. The proposed approach optimizes the process parameters with the objectives of minimizing the geometric inaccuracy and maximizing the filament bond quality of the manufactured part. First, experiments are conducted to collect data pertaining to the part quality. Then, Bayesian neural network (BNN) models are constructed to predict the geometric inaccuracy and bond quality as functions of the process parameters. The BNN model captures the model uncertainty caused by the lack of knowledge about model parameters (neuron weights) and the input variability due to the intrinsic randomness in the input parameters. Using the stochastic predictions from these models, different robustness-based design optimization formulations are investigated, wherein process parameters such as nozzle temperature, nozzle speed, and layer thickness are optimized under uncertainty for different multi-objective scenarios. Epistemic uncertainty in the prediction model and the aleatory uncertainty in the input is considered in the optimization. Finally, Pareto surfaces are constructed to estimate the tradeoffs between the objectives. Both the BNN models and the effectiveness of the proposed optimization methodology are validated using the actual manufacturing of the parts.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-Level Bayesian Calibration of a Multi-Component Dynamic System Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18430)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan,Shunsaku Matsumoto,Yoshitomo Miyagi,Daigo Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层级贝叶斯校准方法，用于融合异构数据源并考虑建模与测量中的不确定性，以实现对时变多组件系统的校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决多组件系统中来自不同时间点和组件的异构数据融合问题，并有效处理建模与测量中的不确定性。

Method: 采用多层级贝叶斯校准方法，分组件级和系统级进行参数估计，通过顺序融合数据并采用迭代策略实现离线和在线两种校准模式。

Result: 该方法在燃气轮机转子叶片热机械行为分析中验证了有效性，能够准确校准参数并修正模型预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的多层级贝叶斯校准方法能有效融合异构时序数据，合理量化多层次不确定性，提升多组件系统的建模精度，适用于离线与在线应用场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a multi-level Bayesian calibration approach that fuses information from heterogeneous sources and accounts for uncertainties in modeling and measurements for time-dependent multi-component systems. The developed methodology has two elements: quantifying the uncertainty at component and system levels, by fusing all available information, and corrected model prediction. A multi-level Bayesian calibration approach is developed to estimate component-level and system-level parameters using measurement data that are obtained at different time instances for different system components. Such heterogeneous data are consumed in a sequential manner, and an iterative strategy is developed to calibrate the parameters at the two levels. This calibration strategy is implemented for two scenarios: offline and online. The offline calibration uses data that is collected over all the time-steps, whereas online calibration is performed in real-time as new measurements are obtained at each time-step. Analysis models and observation data for the thermo-mechanical behavior of gas turbine engine rotor blades are used to analyze the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [158] [Process Optimization Under Uncertainty for Improving the Bond Quality of Polymer Filaments in Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18431)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Matthew Sato,Sankaran Mahadevan,Paul Witherell*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在不确定性下优化熔融沉积成型（FFF）工艺参数的计算框架，通过结合瞬态热传导分析和烧结颈生长模型来最大化聚合物丝材间的粘结质量，并利用Sobol指数进行方差敏感性分析，引入高斯过程代理模型考虑模型差异，最终通过实验验证了优化结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对FFF制造零件的质量主动控制，需要准确预测过程中的变异性，但现有模型受假设和近似影响存在不确定性，因此需系统量化这些不确定性并将其纳入工艺优化中。

Method: 耦合瞬态热传导分析与烧结颈生长模型评估丝材界面粘结质量；采用基于Sobol指数的方差敏感性分析量化不同不确定性源的影响；构建高斯过程（GP）代理模型以包含模型差异，并在优化中考虑不确定性和模型偏差；通过物理实验进行模型校准与验证。

Result: 成功量化了多种不确定性源（包括随机性和认知性）对粘结质量预测的影响，所提出的含模型差异的优化方法显著提升了相邻丝材间的粘结质量，实验验证了优化方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 将不确定性与模型差异纳入工艺参数优化可有效提高FFF过程中聚合物丝材的粘结质量，为实现高质量增材制造提供了可靠的预测与控制方法。

Abstract: This paper develops a computational framework to optimize the process parameters such that the bond quality between extruded polymer filaments is maximized in fused filament fabrication (FFF). A transient heat transfer analysis providing an estimate of the temperature profile of the filaments is coupled with a sintering neck growth model to assess the bond quality that occurs at the interfaces between adjacent filaments. Predicting the variability in the FFF process is essential for achieving proactive quality control of the manufactured part; however, the models used to predict the variability are affected by assumptions and approximations. This paper systematically quantifies the uncertainty in the bond quality model prediction due to various sources of uncertainty, both aleatory and epistemic, and includes the uncertainty and the model discrepancy in the process parameter optimization. Variance-based sensitivity analysis based on Sobol indices is used to quantify the relative contributions of the different uncertainty sources to the uncertainty in the bond quality. A Gaussian process (GP) surrogate model is constructed to compute and include the model discrepancy within the optimization. Physical experiments are conducted for calibration and validation of the physics model and also for validation of the optimum solution. The results show that the proposed formulation for process parameter optimization under uncertainty results in high bond quality between adjoining filaments of the FFF product.

</details>


### [159] [Component-wise hyperreduction for nonlinear solid mechanics problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18868)
*Stephan Ritzert,Jannick Kehls,Erik Faust,Hagen Holthusen,Stefanie Reese,Tim Brepols*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于组件级超减法的可重用非线性固体力学建模范式，通过POD和ECSW方法在离线阶段生成降阶模型，并利用磨床网格绑定连接子结构，实现了高精度和高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高非线性固体力学仿真的效率和模块化程度，需要开发能够在不同装配、边界条件、网格、材料参数和本构模型之间重复使用的降阶模型组件。

Method: 采用组件级别的适当正交分解（POD）和能量守恒采样加权（ECSW）方法，结合磨床网格绑定技术连接子结构；离线计算POD模态和ECSW权重及元素，每个子结构引入12个刚体运动模式处理有限刚体运动。

Result: 数值算例表明，在大规模准静态装配中误差低于1%，且仅需评估不到10%的元素；相同的组件基底和ECSW元素成功应用于有限应变粘弹性动力学，尽管它们仅基于弹性Neo-Hookean组件模拟训练得到。

Conclusion: 组件级超减法能够为模块化的非线性固体力学仿真提供可重用的降阶构建块，具有良好的通用性和计算效率。

Abstract: Hyperreduced nonlinear solid-mechanics components can be generated offline and reused as transferable building blocks across different assemblies, boundary conditions, meshes, material parameters, and constitutive models. We use proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) and energy conserving sampling and weighting (ECSW) on the component level and connect the substructures by mortar mesh tying. The POD modes and the ECSW weights and elements are computed offline from simulations of single components and the finite rigid body motions are treated by 12 additional rigid body modes per substructure. The numerical examples demonstrate errors below 1 \% for large quasi-static assemblies while evaluating less than 10 \% of the elements. The same component bases and ECSW elements are successfully reused for finite-strain viscoelastic dynamics, although they were trained only on elastic Neo-Hookean component simulations. These results indicate that component-wise hyperreduction can provide reusable reduced building blocks for modular nonlinear solid-mechanics simulations.

</details>


### [160] [Image Generation Techniques for Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18908)
*Katharina Roth,Eva Hagen,Alexander Bartscher,Inga Scheler,Nicolas R. Gauger*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于掩码加权条件流匹配（MWCFM）的生成式AI模型，用于自动生成符合城市规划需求的建筑表现图，强调模型优化与风格一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高城市规划中建筑表现图生成的效率与准确性，需要一种能够聚焦关键空间元素并遵循特定风格指南的自动化方法。

Method: 提出Mask-based Weighted Conditional Flow Matching (MWCFM)，在Flow Matching基础上引入上下文掩码以实现对关键特征的精确关注，并将风格指南通过损失函数融入训练过程，使用城市街景数据进行训练。

Result: 模型能有效生成符合城市规划要求和风格规范的建筑表现图像，并通过与应用相关的指标进行性能评估。

Conclusion: MWCFM方法提升了生成图像在复杂 urban 场景中的相关性和视觉质量，为建筑设计可视化提供了高效、可定制的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: In the context of urban planning, architects are normally instructed with creating presentation images that visualize proposed buildings within their urban context. This work aims to develop a GenAI model for automatically generating architectural presentation images in urban scenes, with emphasis on model optimization. To achieve this, we developed Mask-based Weighted Conditional Flow Matching (MWCFM), which extends Flow Matching by introducing contextual masks for precise feature focusing. This enables targeted training on critical spatial elements relevant to urban planning. Our trained model learns from urban street-view data while adhering to specific style-guidelines, which are integrated into training through the loss function. Furthermore, the model's performance is evaluated using application related metrics, derived from presentation image style guidelines.

</details>


### [161] [U.S. National Liquefaction Hazard Maps and their Implications for Engineering Practice and Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19137)
*Morgan D. Sanger,Victoria P. Zdanovski,Brett W. Maurer*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用力学感知的地理空间机器学习模型，生成了美国本土高分辨率的液化灾害图，结合2023年国家地震危险性模型，分析了条件与非条件液化风险，并探讨了震级选择、风险表述形式及社会经济脆弱性对液化风险的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为改进现有建筑规范中单一情景液化风险评估的局限性，提供更全面、理性的全国性液化危险性评估工具，并支持土地利用政策、区域地震响应规划和监管决策。

Method: 采用基于原位测试数据校准的地理空间机器学习模型，替代传统液化模型，结合大量地理空间预测因子推断地下条件，并与2023年美国国家地震危险性模型卷积，在约90米分辨率下绘制条件（2475年重现期）和非条件（地面失效重现期）液化风险图。

Result: 发现震级选择（众数与均值）显著影响条件风险计算结果；非条件风险图揭示了被单一场景地图掩盖的重要空间差异；存在虽小但统计显著的社会经济不平等现象，表现为弱势群体更易暴露于液化风险中。

Conclusion: 所提出的国家液化危险性地图提供了比现行建筑规范更合理、连续的空间风险视图，支持多领域应用，并揭示了工程实践和政策制定中需关注的关键问题，包括风险表征方式的选择和社会公平性考量。

Abstract: This study introduces U.S. national liquefaction hazard maps (NLHMs) developed using a mechanics-informed, geospatial machine learning model which surrogates state-of-practice liquefaction models, exploits a large library of geospatial predictors to infer subsurface conditions, and is anchored to measured conditions with in-situ test data. By convolving this geospatial liquefaction model with the 2023 U.S. national seismic hazard model, liquefaction hazard is mapped across the contiguous U.S. at ~90 m resolution within both conditional (2,475-year design event) and unconditional (return period of ground failure) formulations using high-performance computing for the high-resolution magnitude-disaggregation. The resulting NLHMs provide insights for land-use policy, preliminary site assessment, regional-scale earthquake simulation and response planning, and screening tools for regulatory enforcement, among other applications. Beyond quantifying and visualizing liquefaction hazard, the NLHMs are used herein to examine three questions of engineering practice and policy across a continuous spatial domain: (i) the effect of selecting modal versus mean magnitude in conditional analyses; (ii) the differences between conditional and unconditional hazard formulations; and (iii) the extent to which liquefaction hazard compounds with socioeconomic vulnerability. Results elucidate where and how the choice of magnitude alters computed hazards; that unconditional maps reveal important spatial deviations suppressed by single-scenario maps, which are convenient and widely used in current building codes, but less than completely rational; and that modest but statistically significant socioeconomic gradients in liquefaction exposure exist.

</details>


### [162] [Network-Scale Road Disruption from Liquefaction in Cascadia Subduction Zone Earthquakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19143)
*M. D. Sanger,O. Blaze-Smith,B. W. Maurer,L. Wotherspoon,M. O. Eberhard,J. W. Berman*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种力学信息与数据驱动相结合的框架，用于模拟卡斯卡迪亚俯冲带9级地震引发的液化对道路网络的影响，结合地质空间模型和物理地震动数据，通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估道路中断风险，结果表明影响集中在沿海低地，且相比以往评估，液化导致的桥梁关闭预测显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地评估卡斯卡迪亚俯冲带大地震后液化对交通网络的影响，克服传统地质筛查方法的不足，提升交通韧性规划的科学依据。

Method: 采用基于37,000多个锥探试验训练的地质空间液化模型，结合地貌、水文、气候和表层地质数据，并使用基于物理的地震动情景，通过经验脆弱性关系和空间相关蒙特卡洛模拟传播路段封闭概率。

Result: 结果显示道路中断风险主要集中在沿海低地、河口和河谷地带，尤其是101号国道沿线；在华盛顿州太平洋县和格雷港县分析显示医疗隔离风险升高；仅由液化引起的桥梁关闭预测比以往评估低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该框架通过更精确的地下条件和破坏机制表征，为卡斯卡迪亚地区的交通韧性规划和资产优先级排序提供了可靠工具，尽管存在不确定性，但仍具实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a mechanics-informed, data-driven framework for modeling liquefaction-induced disruption of roadway networks following a magnitude-9 earthquake on the Cascadia Subduction Zone (CSZ). Liquefaction hazard is predicted using a geospatial liquefaction model trained on more than 37,000 cone penetration tests (CPTs) and conditioned on spatial data describing geomorphology, hydrology, climate, and surficial geology. Ground motions are derived from physics-based ensembles of CSZ rupture scenarios. Segment-level probabilities of closure and service degradation are estimated using empirically derived fragility relationships and propagated through the National Highway System using spatially correlated Monte Carlo simulation. Results indicate strong concentration of impacts in coastal lowlands, estuaries, and river valleys, with pronounced exposure along U.S. Route 101. Focused analysis in Pacific and Grays Harbor Counties, Washington, shows elevated probability of healthcare isolation. Compared with prior statewide assessments based on geologic screening, predicted bridge closures due solely to liquefaction are an order of magnitude lower, reflecting improved representation of subsurface conditions and damage mechanisms. Despite uncertainties, the framework provides a defensible tool for transportation resilience planning and asset prioritization in Cascadia.

</details>


### [163] [Sweep-based, implicit solutions of the multidimensional BGK equation on unstructured grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19150)
*Thomas M. Evans,Ryan Glasby,Cory Hauck,Stefan R. Schnake,Kyle J. Schwiebert,Lawton Shoemake,Stuart Slattery*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非结构化网格的高阶间断Galerkin方法求解BGK动理学方程，结合隐式扫掠求解器与矩保持投影，实现时间上的高阶精度和高效并行计算，在GPU上获得显著加速，并在前沿超算上实现了超大规模模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统方法在复杂几何和边界层导致的小时间步长限制，需发展适用于多维非结构网格、兼具高阶精度与高效性的BGK方程数值方法。

Method: 采用节点型间断Galerkin法离散BGK方程，结合基于扫描的隐式求解器和Maxwellian源项的矩保持投影；使用B稳定的对角隐式Runge-Kutta法实现时间高阶精度；通过空间域分解实现混合并行，局部扫描在CPU和GPU上执行，并基于Kokkos库开发可移植的图基扫描算法。

Result: 在2D/3D非结构网格上成功验证了Sod激波问题的连续极限解；展示了线性L2稳定性；在NVIDIA H100 GPU上相较64核AMD CPU实现20倍以上加速；在Frontier超算6144块AMD MI250X GPU上完成了含2.77万亿相空间自由度的边值问题模拟。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在复杂几何下高效、高精度地求解BGK方程，具备良好的可扩展性和硬件可移植性，适合在现代异构超级计算机上进行大规模动理学模拟。

Abstract: We present a nodal discontinuous Galerkin method for solving the Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook (BGK) kinetic equation on multi-dimensional, unstructured grids. The method uses implicit, sweep-based solvers and a moment-preserving projection of the Maxwellian source to enable high-order accuracy in time while avoiding restrictive time steps imposed by boundary layers and other geometry-induced features. We verify that the method is correct in the continuum limit by comparing to closed-form and high-order solutions of the Sod shock problem on 2 and 3D unstructured grids. Linear L2 stability is demonstrated for a B-stable diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta method of third order. The solver uses a hybrid parallel scheme based on spatial domain decomposition with local sweeps performed on CPU and GPU hardware. Platform-portability is demonstrated through the development of new GPU-friendly, graph-based sweep algorithms that are implemented using the Kokkos performance portability library and achieve greater than 20 times speedup on NVIDIA H100 GPUs compared to 64-core AMD EPYC 9654 CPUs. Finally, we show results on the Frontier supercomputer at the Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility for a boundary value problem with 2.77 trillion phase space degrees of freedom that executed on 1536 nodes utilizing 6144 AMD MI250X GPUs.

</details>

### [164] [Searching for symmetric mass generation with staggered fermions in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18239)
*Nouman Butt,Simon Catterall,Gwen Hartshaw,Anna Hasenfratz*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟研究了一个由两个无质量交错费米场和一个标量场组成的格点希格斯模型的相图与临界行为，发现了一个可能对应于多临界点的合并点，并推测其可能描述了一种由特定拓扑缺陷凝聚构成的相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在全局SU(2)对称性下，无质量费米场与伴随表示标量场耦合系统的相结构和临界现象，检验弱耦合与强耦合展开的预测一致性。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，在固定四次耦合的情况下扫描二维参数空间，分析系统的相变行为及边界临界线的合并特性。

Result: 发现了弱耦合下的无质量对称相和强耦合下的有质量对称相（SMG相），两者之间被一个中间反铁磁相分隔；边界临界线在一个特殊点合并，该点处所有费米子双线性凝聚消失但费米子磁化率随格点尺寸以非平凡幂律发散。

Conclusion: 该合并点可能是一个多临界点，暗示存在由某种拓扑缺陷凝聚形成的新奇量子相。

Abstract: We conduct numerical simulations to map out the phase diagram and critical behavior of a lattice Higgs model composed of two massless staggered fermion fields forming a doublet under a global $SU(2)$ and coupled to a scalar field in the adjoint representation of the group. The scalar action consists of a potential comprising quadratic and quartic terms and a scalar kinetic term. At fixed quartic coupling we explore a two-dimensional parameter space finding a massless symmetric phase at weak coupling and a massive symmetric phase (SMG phase) at strong coupling. An intermediate anti-ferromagnetic phase separates these two regimes. These results are consistent with leading order weak and strong coupling expansions. We find that the critical lines bounding the intermediate phase merge at a unique point where all fermion bilinear condensates vanish but fermion susceptibilities diverge as non-trivial powers of the lattice size. We conjecture that this merged point corresponds to a multicritical point and may describe a phase consisting of a condensate of certain topological defects.

</details>
